# The 33 — Rooms 1 to 16 (Bindu → Echo)

*World design for the upgrade, stage 2. Written 13 Oct 2026 from the slice `waves/upgrade/index/by-world/the-33-rooms-1-16.jsonl` (982 rows, every row read), cross-read against `the-33-general.jsonl` where a room's own rows live there (room 6 Vishuddha, room 1 arrival/gate text, chakras 3–8 practice/sound/gate rows), the register (`05-chakra-corpus.md`, `06-chakra-skills.md`, `08-dances.md`, `15-airtable-map.md`, `20-code-play-rooms.md`), the baked canon (`/home/claude/chakra-upload/extracted/uploads/walk-canon.json` v1.0, baked from base `app248ZTWhYJlvQj2`, table `tblwpmyKOeMxEEHd7`), the app bake `iOS/Bindu Mandala/Data/ChakraConstellation.json`, and `waves/register/audio-on-github.md`.*

**What this file is not.** The shell every room stands in — the five threads and their unseal rule, the gate engines, the sound engine, the pulse layer, the Road and the constellation, crossings through the point, the menu and back-to-where-you-were — is designed once in **`waves/upgrade/worlds/the-33-architecture.md`** (written in parallel). This file names those parts and never redefines them. It designs what each of the sixteen rooms *is* inside that shell: its identity, its moving point, its practice, its wake-up, its dance, its gate, its teaching and its doors, with every number.

**Crossed field names (read this before using any source).** The Airtable Map and the baked walk-canon name the same fields differently (register 15 A.0). This file always uses the **Map** name, with the walk-canon key in brackets where a source quotes it:

| Map field (id) | walk-canon key | what it is in the room |

| Leela Story `flduxpB6EM5tFRxUe` | `gate.gate_text` | the **after-gate text**, revealed once the offering has gone (a letter only at 9) |

## 1 · The world

### 1.1 What it is

**In his words.** "The Bindu conceptualizes as a starting point and then it explodes itself … from the point of not knowing. So first it explodes its own energies … and through that it gets to know itself, it gets to remember … it gets to the crown and in that space it sort of like knows itself … then … it goes into the shadow corridor and that is where uncertainty arises … the corridor to be born into the body … the body has a journey of learning about the feet, learning about the breath, learning about the forge … If you play with the body, the body plays with you … The body has a feeling, let it feel through." (C-1327, 25 Apr 2026; index G3-0500.) "The seven chakras … require … a stillness so the energy can sort of move … these chakras your eyes and your heart they require like movement, they require activation … shake it fast enough and in that shaking your heart generates a euphoria pulse … ears = echo chakra … the vishuddha chakra … silence … the mouthpiece is the roar." (C-1311; G3-0491.) "Feel it in your hands, in your fingers … in your feet, in your toes … Once you have felt it through, now comes recalibration." (C-1145; G3-0385.)

**In Design's words (the Bindu Chakras App room registers, G3-0264 and each room's BCA line).** "Warm gold on cream; a single breathing dot is the entire scene … the only room with no past … Bindu is the weather" (1). "The heaviest room in the app. Everything settles downward" (2). "Nothing in this room is still" (3). "Shortest sentences, fastest tempo … it hurts a little. That's fair" (4). "The hinge of the climb — the first room that asks nothing and offers everything" (5). "The only room whose shape constricts and releases" (6). "Subtraction as form — the dimmest energy room" (7). "Everything the previous six rooms taught as doing is inverted into receiving" (8). Shadow: "the pulse begins here" (9). "The only room designed to be walked barefoot" (10). Forge "the only one whose gesture is not visible" (11). Prana "the mic listens to actual breath" (12). Hands "multi-touch palms on glass" (13). Pulse "the only Tap Rhythm gate" (14). Sonar "speak aloud, one outgoing ripple per utterance" (15). Echo "make NO sound … the only gate asking presence only" (16).

**So the sixteen rooms are three movements.** The **Bindu** (1) is the origin and the weather: one point on cream. The **Soul Tree** (2–8, the seven energy rooms, gesture family *Stillness*, holds shortening 3500 → 1800 ms as the walk rises, Bhupura → 16-petal lotus → 8-petal lotus) is the point discovering its own energies from ground to crown. **Shadow** (9, *Recognition*, a threshold not a room-in-the-body) is the corridor into the body, where the pulse begins. The **Bindu Tree** body rooms 10–16 (*Movement*, āvaraṇas 4–6) are the body learning itself: feet, furnace, breath, hands, heartbeat, voice sent, sound received — each with an instrument that reads the body (pressure, mic, taps). Rooms 17–19 finish the body section in the other half of this world (not here).

**The Foundation and the rulings this file applies.** The 33 is "the braid: story told by a moving point, practice, wake-up, dance, gate" (READER-BRIEF-2). Ruling 5: energies, not stories — every room's prose layers (Act 1, Act 2, Bindu Reflection, Witness, Story Thread, the letters) go to **Bindu Feed** (§6); the room keeps the motion, the practice, the sound, the gesture and the teaching. Ruling 6: the dance names every archetype. Ruling 12: songs are his recordings on GitHub. Ruling 13: positions stay. Ruling 14: Vision's mirror door → Manipura (lands in room 4 as an incoming one-way door). Ruling 15: after-effect felt, never named. Ruling 16: app-added limits lifted (no sequential gating). Ruling 17: everything shown word for word, revealed through each room's form, never as a page. Settled: App Activity is the single ledger; no counts about him; Design's lines count as authored; Mandala/Map colours win; each room keeps its fixed tone and a song's drone plays only during its dance; one tuning (Design's ring roots 55.00 · 61.74 · 69.30 · 73.42 · 82.41 · 92.50 · 98.00 · 110.00 · 123.47 Hz, G1-0277); no generated voice; no figure of a Śakti (the walker's own body outline is allowed — see §7 C-07).

### 1.2 The room, as one experience (what every one of the sixteen does, in the architecture's shell)

Each room runs the same six moments, built from the architecture's parts; the room fills each with its own material (sections R01–R16):

1. **Arrival** — the crossing blooms from the touch in the room's Map colour (architecture: crossings). The ground settles to the room's look. First visit: 2 BREATHs of bare ground → the point condenses over 2.4 BREATHs → the **Story Portal Headline** fades in (1 BREATH) → the **Core Question** (1.6 BREATHs) → the **Hero Threshold** seed (1.4 BREATHs) → the walker still-touches the point for 1 BREATH within 24 px (the room's bed rises .004 + p×.008 while touching) → haptic 20 ms → the point blinks → 0.6 BREATH → the room is open. A returning walker gets a single BREATH. **BREATH = 5000 ms × pace** in every room (the shell's only unit of time, G2-0481). This is room 1's built arrival (G2-0485, `shell/bindu-slice-a.js`) applied to all sixteen with each room's own three lines. Lines: Lora italic, line-height 1.85, cream on dark grounds / ink #3A2E15 on cream; 0.9 s ease cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1) in.
2. **Story-as-motion** — the point tells the room's story by moving: the room's authored scene states (from the V5 practice pages and the panel scenes) play on one DotStage canvas, tap- or hold-advanced, with **no words** except the **Signature Phrase** once at the end (800 ms after the last beat, held 2.2 s). Then the **Veil** (#060308; two eyes open after 700 ms, blink once; "press and hold to enter" at 2900 ms; ring r 32 fills over 1800 ms; early release resets in 400 ms; dissolves 600 ms) and the Act-2 field (room colour → cream → silver-white; 26 particles 2–7 px rising over 18–44 s) carries **only the last line of Act 2**, engraved (Lora 38–56 px, weight 500): "Go." / "Follow." / … — then the next-colour door. The words of Act 1 and Act 2 are Feed material (§6).
3. **Practice** — the room's held instrument with its breath ratio, its numbers, its Map Practice Prompt revealed one line per held beat (gesture G1 hold-to-read: 560 ms/word, ×1.5 on heart words, +440 ms after . ; : ! ?, +210 ms after a comma), the **Writing Prompt** as an offering field (lined, auto-growing, saved 900 ms after the last keystroke, "Saved" for 2 s; the **Writing Reflection** fades in once there is text), the **mudrā for three breaths**, and **Three Ways In** as three doors out.
4. **Wake-up** — the room's fixed awakening tone, its binaural pair, its sounding (Mirror Frequency Instruction), its body point and word, its release timeline and the next colour bleeding up from the bottom edge (Tree-of-Life panel grammar, G3-0191).
5. **Dance** — the room's song (his recording) under the Score player with the named ensemble; "watch" invites at 35 s of the wake-up release.
6. **Gate** — the room's own instrument (Canvas · Textarea · Sound Response · Tap Rhythm · Device Tilt), its **Gate Text** word for word, the offering lifting away ("The room keeps what you gave it. You walk lighter."), then the **after-gate text** (Map *Leela Story*) revealed word by word, then the **Teaching Text** paragraph by held paragraph, then **"What the [Name] Knows That You Forgot"** (Post-Engagement Teaching).

Between threads movement is free; within a thread the rooms go in Road order; a room's gate unseals when its other four threads have been walked (architecture: the braid). The gate glyph says "The gate is warm." when open, "The gate knows how to wait too." while waiting (G7-0452); nothing locks, nothing counts.

### 1.3 Visual language

- **Colour.** The Map Color Hex is the room (Mandala/Map colours win); the Primary Color hex is its deep ground; body rooms add their Colour Dyad as the second light. All 16 values are in each room's identity table. Every other hex in the sources (Field hues, homepage palette A, ROAD_CHAKRAS, CHAKRA_HUES, panel grounds) is subordinate (§7 C-03).
- **Chassis.** The chakra chassis (rupakarshini chassis B, G5-0346/G5-0486/G5-0520): one colour table behind every surface; night ground #0C0A12 (to #020205) for energy and body rooms; **cream #F8F1DF** (surface #EADCBA, ink #3A2E15, gold #C9A84C, glow #E8C568) for the Bindu room; Shadow's void #0a0a12 / #050508. Type: Cormorant Garamond light wide-tracked caps for names; Lora italic for every revealed line; DM Sans 8–10 px uppercase .16–.42 em for the few labels. Motion budget: breathe · emerge · turn (orb breath 4.0 s, scale 1→1.07; emerge 12 px, stagger .06 s; turn 80 s / 50 s).
- **The breathing yantra.** Behind every room a faint Śrī Yantra (4 apex-up, 5 apex-down triangles, bindu r 1.4, circles r 88 and r 90) breathes opacity .035 → .058 and scale 1 → 1.025 over 14 s (G5-0650): the 33 visibly stand on her; aniconic.
- **The persistent red Bindu.** A 6 px dot #C0392B, top 16 / right 16, opacity .3, in every room and every dance, never explained; it "awakens" at 32 s of each wake-up release and invites "watch" at 35 s (G3-0077, G3-0322).
- **Weather.** The ground wears the story thread's sky (dark at Shadow); the next room's hex bleeds in at the horizon (G2-0912).

### 1.4 Sound (values per room; engine in the architecture)

Every room has five layers, never more than the architecture's sound engine allows at once: **(a)** the **Om floor** 136.1 Hz with partials 272.2 (×.3) and 68.05 (×.15), gain .006–.01, LFO .05 Hz ±.008 — a floor, never a crescendo (G2-0482, G7-0728); **(b)** the **crossing tone** = the ring root of the room's āvaraṇa (one tuning): ring 1 55.00 Hz (rooms 2–4), ring 2 61.74 (5, 8), ring 3 69.30 (6, 7), ring 4 73.42 (10, 11, 15), ring 5 82.41 (12, 13, 14), ring 6 92.50 (16), ring 9 123.47 (1); Shadow has none — the 58 Hz pulse takes its place; **(c)** the room's **fixed awakening tone** at the wake-up gate (174 / 210 / 320 / 341 / 384 / 426 / 480 Hz for 2–8; the twin's tone for 10–16; none for 1 and 9); **(d)** the **binaural pair** of the wake-up (per room); **(e)** the **song and its absent-fifth carrier**, only inside the dance. From room 9 the **pulse** (60 bpm haptic at the bottom of the glass + 58 Hz glow arc) runs under everything (architecture: pulse).

## R01 · BINDU — The Origin

### Identity (The Map, record `recKAQWX7yUIB3ktW`)

| field | value (verbatim) |

| Colours | Map hex **#E8C568** · Primary "Gold (#C9A84C)" · ground cream #F8F1DF · the persistent dot #C0392B |
| System · tree · stage | Tree of Life · Structural · Origin · activation Recognition · gate **Canvas** (the only Canvas gate in the walk) |

| Śakti | "Lalita Mahatripurasundari herself / Rajarajeshvari — presiding consciousness of Avarana 9 (Sarvanandamaya Chakra). The Bindu IS the Shakti. Three sub-bindus at center: Kurukulla (red, feminine), Varahi (white, masculine), and their union (the individual soul)." |

### What renders today

`ChakraRoomView.swift:84-132` shows room 1 as a vertical scroll of Map columns: the "triangle" kind look shared with the summit rooms (`ChakraRoomDoor.swift:60-145`), Act 1 and four story layers as text, the Practice Prompt as text, a wake-up with no syllable and no button, no dance, and the Canvas gate turned into a **text field plus the one 1.4 s capsule hold** every gate shares (G7-0362, G7-0352). An entry tone of 110 Hz at .05 plays on entering (G7-0358). Game doors: Sri Yantra, World in a Box, Leela. The room is reached from the constellation drawn into the Mandala canvas (G7-0068) — to be moved into this world (architecture). **Outside the app, room 1 is the one room Design fully built** (`shell/`): the Held Threshold, the arrival, the room with three doors, the wordless five-movement story (slice B), Practice v3 (eight movements), the still-touch wake-up and the Canvas gate (G2-0480, G2-0848–0850, G2-0916–0917). This room is a port, then more.

### Arrival

- **Way in:** Road from Home (the point), the constellation's centre light, the Mandala's centre (kp 102 door), the Leela landing on square 1, the mirror door from Integration (29), the menu. The crossing blooms in **#E8C568** and settles to cream.
- **First time ever in the 33 — the Held Threshold** (built, `shell/bindu-slice-a.js 1–301`): cream; the seed line; rings. "headphones, if you have them" · "press, and hold — one full breath". The walker presses one BREATH (5 s × pace); rings grow, the seed line pulses; on completion the SilenceEngine unlocks, Om floor to .006, wake lock, haptic 30 ms, the body settles (G2-0484, G2-0848).
- **Every arrival** (built, G2-0485): 2 BREATHs empty cream → the gold point (#C9A84C, radial #E8C568 → #C9A84C, shadow rgba(201,168,76,.65)) condenses from ~90 motes over 2.4 BREATHs → **"something is listening on the other side"** (1 BREATH) → **"What is here before everything else?"** (1.6 BREATHs) → **"Before the tree. Before the name. Before the game. This."** (1.4 BREATHs; the legacy hero breathes this line over 12 s, G2-0498) → the walker still-touches the dot for one BREATH within 24 px (Om rises .004 + p×.008 while held; a still finger cancels beyond 24 px) → haptic 20 → blink → 0.6 BREATH → the room. A returning walker: one BREATH. The React variant's beats (1.8 / 4.6 / 9.4 / 13.8 s, still-touch 2.6 s within 14 px, or the 9 s wait variant, G2-0492) remain the **reduce-motion / no-touch path**.
- **The room** (built, G2-0486, `bindu-room.jsx 113–198`): the dot moves to (.5, .26), wander 3; the signature line shows faint; three cream doors arrive one breath apart — "The story / in Bindu's voice", "The practice / one line at a time", "One sound. / there is no sound yet" — the braid's stations for this room (dance and gate are reached from the wake-up and from the gate glyph). Leaving: left-edge drag or Esc, "never with 'are you sure?'" (G5-0212).

### Story-as-motion

The **wordless five-movement story on one DotStage** (built, `shell/bindu-slice-b.js`; G2-0490; the room's Act 1 words go to Feed, §6):
1. **Flicker** — the dot nearly goes out twice.
2. **Forms fail** — it tries place, body, thought; three bells 151, 97, 211 Hz; the flicker stops; two words engraved: JUST: HERE.
3. **Aurora** — curious behaviour, comfort .17, three strains.
4. **Covenant** — dust freezes; the Sakshi point at (.91, .30) rises to opacity .3; a thread descends and beckons; the walker touches; the dot crosses in 2.8 s; haptic 40 ms; bell 272.2 Hz for 7 s; ripples; a pool.
5. **Dawn** — the background warms to cream over 9 s; only "Go." is engraved; the story thread is marked walked.

Then the Signature Phrase once (800 ms after Dawn, held 2.2 s). The dot's own states from the legacy page (leaning → thread grows 70 px at 38° → wavering → rippling, signature held 2.8 s, G2-0502) are the second-visit variant. The **Veil** and Act 2's last line "**Go.**" close it; the Muladhara door rises edge to edge in **#B83A3A**: "Muladhara / The ground is here. / Press · Continue →" (G2-0505, G3-0163). Fix carried from the prototype: the covenant pool must not overlay text (G2-0491); `wireHold()` gets a once-guard (G2-0506).

### Practice (held)

**Practice v3 — the LivingDot in eight movements** (built, `shell/bindu-practice.jsx 141–550`, G2-0495): Contact → Name (the word splits BIND · U) → Smallest (a 44-walker field) → Gaia (green flood; "the ground is asking — touch it, and stay") → Lalita (zoom; three sub-bindus Kurukulla red / Varahi white / union merge into a triangle) → Recognition (breathing ceases for 10 s) → Way Back (the triangle draws) → **Place the Point**. Each movement advances by touching the dot (DotHit r 34, "touch to continue").

- **The point** (the Map practice, verbatim: "Draw a single point. Tap the canvas once — wherever your hand moves to. Don't deliberate. The deliberating is the thing that will interfere. The hand knows. …"): a 4:3 canvas; one tap places a gold point r 4 (#C9A84C), animating in over 900 ms from 0.3 r, ease-out-cubic, shadow blur 12; stored as his point {nx, ny} (G2-0500, G3-0073). The witness line brightens over 3 s: **"Look at your point. It doesn't look like much. That's how origins work."** (Writing Reflection). Returning: **"Your point. Still here. Place again if you wish."** Clearing removes it.
- **Sound of the practice** (Design's Experience Score, planned, G2-0918): the practice is scored at a 204 Hz fifth, then 171.5 + 204 Hz together, then about 14 s of silence.
- **Mudrā — Yoni Mudra, three breaths of 8 s** (BinduPracticeV1, G2-0494): the Map's hand words revealed one line per breath — "Bring both hands together — thumbs touching, index fingers touching — creating the triangle between them. The womb gesture. The return to source. … Hold it for three breaths." The carried line: "this mudra is the permission slip. One breath inside it brings you back to the point." The Yoni Mudra is the thread through all 32 rooms (G6-0498).
- **Three Ways In** (doors, verbatim heads): "Place another point." (the first disappears) · "Find a point already here." · "Close your eyes and find the point inside. … That's the Bindu. No canvas needed."
- **After the practice** (Practice v3): MudraM → reflection → UnfoldingM → "Leave" / "Go deeper".

### Wake-up

- **The wake-up is a still touch** (built, `bindu-moments.jsx 154–184`, G2-0496): the walker still-touches the point for **7 s**; then **"Recognized."** (Design's line); the wake-up thread is marked walked. Going further (Design's Experience Score, G2-0918): if the finger stays, the hold extends to **~12 s at 44 px tolerance**, the Om floor rising with it.
- **Sounding (Mirror Frequency Instruction, verbatim):** "No hum. No gesture. No body location. / Place one finger on any surface and hold it still. / Feel the single point of contact. / That contact — one point, meeting one point — is Bindu's frequency." Revealed one line per held breath during the touch.
- **Tone:** none — no awakening tone, no binaural pair. The Om floor (136.1 Hz stack) is the only sound; the room's crossing tone is ring 9's root **123.47 Hz** (one tuning).
- **The Wakeup door** (Design copy, G2-0501): Śrī Yantra glyph (4 up / 5 down), "A guided arrival at the point that was always here." ~7 min.
- **Release:** no panel. The next colour bleeds up: **#B83A3A** rises from the bottom edge at 24 s of resting in the room after the wake-up (M-BLEED).

### Dance

- **Door** (Design, G2-0501): lemniscate glyph (animateMotion 4.8 s), "Offer one word. Then let the point move." ~4 min.
- **Input (Canvas room):** the dancers' lead path is **his placed point** — the coordinates of the point he placed in the practice seed Bindu's position (G4-0230).
- **Song (his recording):** **`bindu/still.mp3`** (Ember — "Sit Around The Fire", Ram Dass; 50.6% silence, Karishma dominant). Ruling settled here (§7 C-11): site-v3 stands Meditate pieces at the Bindu ("Meditate → Bindu", G4-0594) and the Field Song 1 of 33 "The note at the origin — where everything in the Field begins" has no file. **Fruit** (verbatim, `still.html`): "You gave this fire “{w}”. And the fire held {w} without burning it. Every pulse was {w} breathing. Every silence was {w} remembering it didn't need to be anything else. You didn't bring {w} to the fire. The fire showed you that {w} was already warm." (G4-0056).
- **Archetypes:** all named — Bindu (lead, doubled size and range, since the room's Voice is Bindu), Gaia, Sid, Arch, Karishma, Sakshi, Ashrey's web, with Shweta, Neev and Lalita by their windows (S.13). Return: the ensemble dissolves in reverse and Bindu spirals to the centre dot — the dance ends at the point (G4-0237).

### Gate — Canvas

- **Gate Text (Map, verbatim):**
  > The canvas is open.
  >
  > One point exists. It is the beginning of everything that follows.
  > Tap anywhere to place it.
  >
  > Button: Place the point
- **Behaviour** (built `BinduGate`, G2-0497; Experience Score, G2-0918): the instruction reveals line by line on cream; the walker taps anywhere — a point is placed (the offering, separate from his practice point); he **holds it 4.6 s** while the Om floor rises toward .05 over ~10 s; on release it **lifts away over 3.4 s** and the Om decays over ~14 s; ● "The room keeps what you gave it. You walk lighter." The offering is written to App Activity, never shown as a count. The glyph shows "The gate is warm." / "The gate knows how to wait too." (G2-0487). Padakarshini's G18 (a spiral traced into the centre, done within 26 units) is the alternate gesture for a walker who traces instead of taps (G4-0817).
- **After-gate text (Map Leela Story, word by word at 420 ms/word, +620 after a full stop, +1200 between paragraphs):**
  > The point has been placed.
  >
  > Everything that happens from here radiates from what you just did. The walk begins not when you press the first chakra but when you felt Bindu for the first time — which you just did.
  >
  > You don't need to understand that yet. You just need to know it happened.
  >
  > Keep moving.

### The teaching, revealed after the offering

- **Teaching Text** (`fldCs6Vg2umNzxyQc`, 2559 ch, 7 ¶): opens "Bindu is not a technique." … closes "The same point." One paragraph per held breath (G1 hold-to-read); the paragraph on Lalitā, Avarana 9 and the three sub-bindus lights three points that merge into a triangle (reusing Practice v3's Lalita movement).
- **"What the Bindu Knows That You Forgot"** (Post-Engagement Teaching `fldC2OAa6NYrxrxWC`, 1403 ch, 6 ¶): opens "You placed it." … closes "The rest is the unfolding." As its paragraphs reveal, **32 spiral points in the future rooms' colours** unfold outward from his point (UnfoldingM + the Experience Score's "spiral points in future room colours"); the last paragraph lands as the 32nd point appears. An unlock flag, never a count.

### Doors

- **Mirror:** → **Integration (29)**, one way; the door shows the Subtle Mirror text as you hold it, then crosses through the point (not this world's room).

## R02 · MULADHARA — Where the Body Meets the Earth

### Identity (The Map, record `recT3JC8Qz9awKo8j`)

| field | value (verbatim) |
|---|---|
| Name · title | Muladhara · Where the Body Meets the Earth · मूलाधार · Muladhara |
| Verb | PRESS (Panel Verb PRESS) |
| Colours | Map hex **#B83A3A** · Primary "Deep earth red (#8B1A1A)" (the wake-up ground) · release line #C0392B |
| System · tree · stage | Energy · Soul Tree · Energy · activation Stillness · gate Textarea |
| Voice | Gaia |
| Śakti | "10 Siddhi Devis — Anima through Sarvakama" (Shakti Function glosses each: Anima infinitely small · Laghima weightless · Mahima infinitely large · Ishitva mastery · Vashitva command · Prakamya irresistible will · Bhukti enjoyment · Ichha pure desire · Prapti obtaining · Sarvakama all desires fulfilled; presiding Tripura, Yoginī Prakata, mental state Jagrata, "Body of the goddess at this avarana: her feet.") |
| Āvaraṇa · seats | Bhupura (Avarana 1, outer ring) · kp **1–10** · ring 1 |
| Mudrā | "Kshobha Mudra — the readiness gesture, entering the three worlds" — hand: "both hands at the heart, fingers interlaced, thumbs pointing up" |
| Bīja | "लं · LAM (the earth seed — yellow square, four petals, sealed and sounding)" |
| Body location | "Base of spine / Earth beneath feet" (page: "Base of the spine · The pelvic floor · Where the body meets the earth") |
| Tree | Soul Tree, all roots: canopy and root system of equal size; LAM in the trunk grain; six Śaktis along the roots; Bhupura of three squares; no sky. Caption: "Tap to look closer. The roots hold more than shows at first." |
| Mirror | **Sahasrara** (8) — "Sahasrara. The two poles of the energy body. … The root holds the crown's question before the question is asked." |
| Core question · seed · panel seed | "Am I safe here?" · "This is what holds you. It was here before you arrived." · "The earth that holds you was chosen by you." (= Signature) |
| Essence | "The ground you stand on. Literally and in every other sense." |
| Codex refs | C-1166, C-1313 |

### What renders today

Five breathing rings (the look all seven energy rooms share), Act 1 and the story layers as text, the Practice Prompt as text plus one held movement (**fear, in waves**, C-1166), the bīja text with a **"sound LAM"** button (`BijaSoundService.playBija(..., duration 4.2)`), no dance, the gate as a text field plus the shared 1.4 s capsule; game door Root System (G7-0363, G7-0359). Outside the app: the legacy practice page `uploads/muladhara-practice(6).html` (5-beat Gaia story, Sakshi "Follow.") and the full wake-up `uploads/muladhara-wakeup(3).html` (P1, binaural 128 → 132 → 130, 174 Hz) are built (G2-0593); the shell's canon-adapter already plays its 174 Hz wake-up for "Day 19" (G2-0852 — the "Day 19" greeting is a count and is dropped, §7).

### Arrival

The crossing blooms in **#B83A3A** and settles to a deep-earth ground (#8B1A1A at the bottom, ember-dark above). **Everything sits low on the screen** — the heaviest room: every line enters from the lower third and settles downward (register ◆ "text low on screen"). Arrival grammar (§1.2) with: **"something reached back from the other side"** → **"Am I safe here?"** → **"This is what holds you. It was here before you arrived."** The verb PRESS stands once in the room colour (0.9 s in, 0.5 s delay). Design's own note: "the only energy room where the answer arrives before the question ('Am I safe here? Yes.')" — so the point is already resting on the ground line before the question appears. Crossing tone: ring 1 root **55.00 Hz**.

### Story-as-motion

The point's journey, from the legacy story scene (G2-0513, G3-0167; Act 1's Gaia words go to Feed): an ember field; the gold dot starts at 20 % height and descends; a **downward tendril** reaches (reach-down) and a **red response rises** to meet it (reach-up); a meeting glow; **8 root paths draw** with 0–1.0 s staggered delays; the dot passes through **reaching → meeting → settling → deepening → rooted → rooting → rippling**. Five beats, each advanced not by a tap but by **bearing down** (gesture G5, G4-0729): pressure builds at dt·(0.55 + pressure·1.1)/s and falls at 1.5/s; below 0.12 nothing marks ("a quiet line says so"); every 0.9 s at depth d a section strikes and stays (scale .985 + .015d, drop-shadow 0.6 + 1.4d px); a vertical gauge at the edge. On iPhone, pressure = the touch's contact radius plus hold time. Signature **"The earth that holds you was chosen by you."** → the Veil (eyes; 1.8 s ring) → Act 2's last line, engraved: **"Follow."** → the Svadhisthana door rises in **#D47438**: "Svadhisthana / The pull is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line (interlude): "A different question is arriving."

### Practice (held)

- **Press, ten breaths** (Map Practice Prompt, one line per held breath): "Stand up. Feet flat — shoes off if possible. / Press into the floor. Not a stomp. Press — the way you press your palm into clay to leave an impression. Full weight, slow, deliberate. Then hold. / Feel the ground press back. / This is physics and it is also Muladhara: you press down, the earth presses up with equal force. You are held not by courtesy but by law. / Ten breaths. Press on each exhale. On each press, let one question surface: which of the 10 Siddhis is latent in me right now? Don't force an answer. The asking is the opening."
- **Numbers:** breath **4·4·4** (register; panel P1 runs 3 × 12 s), ten breaths = 120 s; the walker presses the ground band at the bottom of the glass on each exhale (G5 bearing gauge; haptic tick on each strike); trace quality *heavy*. During the press the ten Siddhi names lie faint along the ground line, one brightening per exhale (Shakti Function glosses as their sub-lines).
- **Body SCAN** (padakarshini, G4-0804/G4-0818): a band of warmth descends the screen top-down (46 svh, 2.6 s per region; held widens to 66 svh; whole screen 140 svh at the end), six region ticks, warmth mixed toward amber.
- **Offering field:** Writing Prompt "What is the ground made of today? Not the floor. Your ground. What are you standing on?" — saved 900 ms after the last keystroke, "Saved" 2 s (G2-0510); Writing Reflection after text: "You named it. Whatever the ground turned out to be — a body, a room, a debt, a habit, a person, a fact — naming it gave it form. And form gives the root something to press against. The press isn't only into the floor. It's into the answer you just wrote."
- **Mudrā:** Kshobha, three breaths: "After: form the Kshobha Mudra — both hands at the heart, fingers interlaced, thumbs pointing up. The readiness gesture. Three breaths here. This is the permission slip you carry forward: I have touched the ground. I am ready for what comes next." (chain 2·3·4: "one gesture, three rings of one shared earth", G6-0499).
- **Three Ways In:** barefoot ten steps · sit directly on the ground · "Inhale through the nose, four counts. Hold, four counts. Exhale through the mouth with a low hum, eight counts. … Four breaths. That's enough." (the 4·4·8 hum is the third door; the practice itself stays 4·4·4 — §7 C-14).
- **Held movement from his Codex — fear, in waves** (built, `ChakraMovementPractice.swift`; C-1166/1168/1180; G7-0151): violet wash → release → prana; three waves; "the shakti energy was doing a cleansing at the muladhara chakra, the fear of survival" (G3-0397) — offered as a fourth door after the practice.
- **Knowledge door** (legacy page): "What the Root Knows That You Forgot" ends "The ground is here. The ground presses back. You are held." — this is the PET (below), opened after the gate.

### Wake-up — Panel 1 PRESS (`muladhara-wakeup(3).html`, PANEL 1 V3.1)

- **Opening hold:** ground **#8B1A1A**; "headphones on" (1.2 s); the seed breathes; "press and hold" (2.5 s); progress bar after 3 s; **hold 3500 ms** (the room's ladder value; the panel was 4000 ms — §7 C-05); 8 SVG roots, trunk and canopy draw with the progress (tree-gate L-system: roots in the first 40 %, trunk #4A2A1A/#6B3D2A). On completion: haptic 80 ms and the **awakening tone 174 Hz**, harmonics [1, .5, .25], sub-bass 87 Hz at .3, a band-passed noise click at 174 Hz (Q 5, 50 ms), an 80 ms delay echo at .15; attack .01, sustain .4, decay 2.5 s — "Deep, heavy, percussive. Earth drum. The ground announces you."
- **THE GROUND:** "Muladhara · The Root / This is your body's conversation with the earth. The part of you that needed to feel safe before it could become anything else. / Below you are roots. Each root holds a force. Tap any that call to you." Six Siddhi nodes, each lighting two root paths: **Animā** "Where did you hide when you were small?" · **Mahimā** "What was the biggest thing you could imagine at age seven?" · **Laghimā** "When did you first feel weightless — completely free?" · **Garimā** "What made you dig in and refuse to move?" · **Prākāmya** "What did you wish for so hard it felt real?" · **Vaśhitva** "What small world did you create that was completely yours?" Two seconds after the first tap, "go deeper" (hold 2000 ms; the screen darkens to .6).
- **DESCENT** (womb #5A0F0F): "You are very small right now. / Someone is holding you. Or: you needed someone to hold you. / What did safety feel like in your body? Not the word. The feeling. Where did it live? / Tap where it lived." He taps his own body outline (no Śakti figure); six roots spread from the tap; **body point 1 {x, y, label 'safety'}** is written; "That point remembers. It always has."
- **GROUND SHIFT:** a deep pulse at 6 s; "The ground you stood on then — at three, at five, at eight — is the same ground under your feet right now." → auto pivot at 3.5 s (haptic 50) → "It never moved." / "Press your feet into the floor. Not as an exercise. As a greeting." / "The earth greets you back. It always has." Continue = hold 2500 ms.
- **PRACTICE:** "Breathe. Into the place you found." 3 × (4 in · 4 hold · 4 out) = 36 s, labelled in / hold / out (haptics 30 / 20 / 30); "The place you found was waiting for that breath."
- **Binaural:** L and R 128 Hz; gain 0 → .12 over 3 s at THE GROUND, +.02 per Siddhi tapped up to .2; R 128 → 132 Hz over 4 s in the descent (4 Hz theta); R → 130 Hz over 8 s in the shift (2 Hz delta); gain .25 → .10 over 42 s through the breath; fades out over 6 s at release.
- **Sounding (Mirror Frequency Instruction):** "Sit on the floor if you can. Press your sit bones down. / Hum LAM — low, deep, as if the sound is coming from below the floor. / Feel the resonance in the base of your spine." (no generated voice; his own hum, and the mic may listen for it).
- **RELEASE** (seconds from release start): 0.5 "Your Muladhara Chakra is now Breathing." (#C0392B) · 1.5 / 4.0 "Your foundation was always stronger than you knew." / "The tree grows from what was planted here." · 9 divider · 12 the seed returns · 15 mini-reveal **"The earth was already holding you. You just told it where."** · 18 luminance (.05) · 21 dim · 24 **#D47438 bleeds up** from the bottom edge · 32 the persistent red Bindu awakens · 35 "watch" → the dance.
- **Door copy** (Design): Wakeup "Press and hold" — "A guided descent to the body that held you at three, at five, at eight." ~7 min. The cloth's QR (#8B1A1A, `/muladhara-wakeup.html`) deep-links here.

### Dance — Iron (Woodkid)

- **Song:** **`tree-of-life/muladhara.mp3`** (Iron; Score *press*, Track 23 `recNK3tCuTLE5Rz6P`). Second door: **`bindu/before.mp3`** (Genesis, Justice — the Map's second linked song; A2 110 Hz carrier, no return phase; Lalita: "Enjoy those first 40 seconds everyone always skips. That isn't the waiting room. That is the whole answer.").
- **Score (worked example `muladhara-dance.html`):** DUR 201.7 s; PEAK_T 188; RETURN_START 15; DARK_RATIO 1.0 ("Full blackout. Earth doesn't spare you."); BPM 129 (seed typed 460 ms/char); 201 hits at .20; MIRROR_TIMES [26, 50, 98, 146, 188]; fracture at the detonation 16.32 s (16 cracks, 1–2 branches); **Bindu climbs** bottom → top between 15 s and PEAK_T; screen shake h.i×12 decaying .85; gravity drop when h.i > .5; dust from below (≤ 200); molten underglow; seismic lines (2–5, 12 segments) when h.i > .6; aftershock half-rings when h.i > .7; "da-DUM war drum" double-pulse haptics [i×30, 40, i×50]; **GROUNDING return** — everything pulls down HT×.4×progress, the end fades to warm earth hsl(12, 30, 6), not black. Movement type **RINGS** (impact; concentric circles; tree-ring residue). Ensemble radii/ghosts: Bindu 7/55, Gaia 5.5/40 (wide sweeps cx + sin(t·.3)·maxR·.95·eb + cos(t·.18)·maxR·.4), Sid 5/30 (orbit maxR·.65 × .55), Arch 5/40 (mirror ×1.6), Karishma 4.5/30 (full screen in silence), Sakshi 4.5/25 (edge to edge maxR·1.05 × .85), Ashrey 4/30 (hue cycles t·12); every position adds gyro offsets scaled by the word's fluidity. Colour: BINDU_HUE from **#B83A3A** (the Score's hue 12 yields to the Map).
- **Seed** (VX): "You chose this." Mirror words: the Muladhara 27 consonant families (e.g. fl → feel · fall · full · fill · flow; st → stay · star · step · stop · stem); V3 set BODY → BLOOD → IRON → STAR → HOME.
- **Fruit** (verbatim): "The iron in your blood is the same iron in the stars. You were never lost. You were planted. The ground was always yours." + motion weave ("You held still." / "You were swaying with the ground." / "You were moving.") + echo weave; the dwell weave is re-voiced or dropped and the count weave is dropped (floor; §7 C-15). Lalita's one closing line: "Enjoy the soldier thinking he's alone. He never was." The room's one word: **Ground**.
- **Sound inside the dance only:** carrier D3 146.8 Hz (absent fifth, presence .27); beat arc (Atlas) Arrival 8 Hz → Descent 6 → Recognition 4.5 → Home 3 (gain .042) → Return 7; Iron's drums sit at 54 Hz (A1).
- **Archetypes:** all named; **Gaia leads** (the room's Voice: doubled size and range).
- **Door copy** (Design): "Iron — Woodkid" — "Offer one word. Then move. The body of the song is the ground the song forgot it was standing on." ~4 min.

### Gate — Textarea, read as the Map's words ask

- **Gate Text (verbatim):**
  > Sit down. Feel the base of your spine — the tailbone, the seat.
  > Press your sit bones into whatever is holding you.
  > When you feel the ground holding you back — tap.
  >
  > Button: I feel the ground holding me
- **Behaviour:** the mechanism follows the words (Map C7 ruling): the lines arrive low on the screen; an optional word may be offered **without a box** (letters appear on the ground as typed); the button is a **3500 ms heavy hold** on the ground band; on completion the word **lays down into the earth over 7.5 s** (padakarshini G33) and lifts as an ember — "The room keeps what you gave it. You walk lighter."
- **After-gate text** (Map *Leela Story*, revealed word by word):
  > You pressed your feet into the ground. The ground pressed back.
  >
  > That is not a metaphor. That is physics happening in your body. Equal force, opposite direction. The ground was holding you before you noticed. The noticing is not what made it happen. The noticing is what made it available.
  >
  > The root has been doing this your whole life — the stabilization, the grounding, the automatic maintenance of your relationship with the earth. You just made it conscious for a moment. Consciousness changes the quality of what it touches.
  >
  > This is what you carry forward: you are held. The holding was always happening. Now you know it is.

### The teaching, revealed after the offering

- **Teaching Text** (2741 ch, 7 ¶): opens "The root doesn't ask whether you're ready, spiritual, or deserving." … closes "This same Mudra will return at Svadhisthana and Manipura — one gesture, three rings of one shared earth." The paragraph naming the ten Siddhis lights the ten seats' points along the ground line as it reads.
- **"What the Root Knows That You Forgot"** (PET, 1408 ch, 8 ¶): opens "You were held before you could hold yourself." … closes "The ground is here. The ground presses back. You are held." (Legacy heading verbatim, G2-0511.)

### What renders today

Five rings, text story, text practice, bīja text + VAM button, no dance, Textarea gate (field + 1.4 s), game door Water Level (G7-0364). Legacy built: `svadhisthana-practice.html` (5-beat story, Sakshi's sentence as the Go-beat), the 7-phase wake-up (binaural 144 → 150 → 148, 210 Hz) and the Crystallize dance canvas (`uploads/svadhisthana-dance(3).html`) (G2-0601). Two legacy bugs to not carry: the story's *flowing* and *dissolving* states had no drawing (G2-0528); the dance read a word key nothing wrote and printed "21th" (G2-0542).

### Arrival

The crossing blooms in **#D47438**; **nothing in this room is ever still** — the ground is a slow current (7 dashed streams drifting left to right at ~0.04 of the width per second; "even the writing field breathes", BCA). Arrival lines: **"something was already reaching for you"** → **"What wants?"** → **"The pull was never random. It was navigating."** (hero; the legacy page's verb reads FOLLOW — the room's verb FLOW stands here). Crossing tone 55.00 Hz (ring 1).

### Story-as-motion

From the legacy scene (G2-0526, G3-0168): **7 dashed streams flowing; five drift-lights orbiting on 26–32 s periods; Ātmā at the centre, barely visible**. The point moves **reaching → trails → flowing (the Karshini names fade in as labels) → dissolving (Ātmā grows to 24 px; her name appears) → orbits-reveal → both-leading → convergence → rippling** (five orbits, five missed-pull lights at the edges, convergence, a 3.8 s ripple to 60×). Each beat advances by **following a ray** (gesture G3, G4-0727): nine rays (groups 3/4/2), press within 70 px of a ray, travel eases .18/frame toward d/(0.94R) and reels back ×.94/frame when released; other rays dim to .34 and pulse 1.9× faster. Signature **"Every desire was drawing you toward yourself."** → Veil → Act 2's last line engraved, the room's Go-beat sentence: **"The pull is about to become heat. This is not a betrayal of the water. The heat is what the water was finding."** → the Manipura door in **#D4A848** (the source's door used Bindu gold #C9A84C by mistake — fixed, G3-0086): "Manipura / The heat is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line: "I'm being shaped by my own heat."

### Practice (held)

- **Follow** (Map Practice Prompt, one line per breath): "Close your eyes. / Ask yourself — right now, today — what do you want? Not what you should want. Not what makes sense. What does the body lean toward before the mind approves or denies it? / Don't judge it. Don't build a plan. Just feel the pull. Let it exist without managing it. / Write it below — the first thing that surfaces, before the editor arrives."
- **Numbers:** breath **4·0·6** (register; "longer release"), trace *fluid*; padakarshini WATER figure (G4-0819): the surface settles as the breath slows — ripple opacity 1 − calm, calm 0 → 1 across the practice, a leaf drifting on it.
- **Offering field:** "Write it below. The first thing that surfaces. Before the editor arrives." (the field itself breathes: its border scales 1 → 1.007 on the room's 4·0·6 cycle) → Writing Reflection: "Did you write the real one? Or the safe one? You know the difference. Your body knows the difference."
- **Mudrā:** Kshobha, three breaths: "This is the same gesture you formed at Muladhara, and it is the gesture you will form at Manipura. The Bhupura is one bounded earth; the readiness extends through all three rings. / Which of the 8 Matrikas is visiting you right now — which emotional intelligence is present in the pull you just named? You don't need to identify her precisely. Only receive what she's carrying." The eight Matrika glosses stand faint on the water while the mudrā is held.
- **Three Ways In:** "Put on any song and let your hips do what they do when nobody's watching." · "Start making something without a plan." · "Take a bath. … earth holds firmly, water holds gently. Both hold. The sacral is the water version of being held."

### Wake-up — Panel 2 FOLLOW

- **Opening hold:** ground of the panel (P2 V2, warm brown #8B4513 → #D4722A); the seed breathes; **hold 3200 ms** (ladder; the panel had 4000); five water rings brighten at thresholds i × .18 of the progress. On completion: **awakening 210 Hz**, harmonics [1, .6, .3, .15], decay 2.8 s — "Warm, rounded, like a wooden bell. Water ripple in sound." + sub 105 Hz at .3, 50 ms band-passed click, 80 ms delay at .15.
- **THE FOLLOW:** "This is the part of you that was pulled. Before reason. Before choice. The magnetic force that drew you toward what you could not yet name. / Six lights are drifting. Follow the ones that call to you." · "trace with your finger". Six lights on their own motion: **kāma** erratic · **shabda** rhythmic · **sparsha** an edge ellipse · **rūpa** slow, high in the centre · **smṛtya** circles back · **ātma** near the centre, trail "bright". A finger within **35 px** follows a light (haptic 25 ms; its name trails; the finger leaves a trail rgba(232,148,58,.15), max 60 points — the only panel that leaves a visible trail). **Two lights followed → "the current"** continues. (Touch-move only; never on hover.)
- **MEMORY CURRENT:** "Between 11 and 20, something found you. Not something you chose. Something that chose you. / What was it? / One word. The thing that pulled you." Button "offer" → **word 2** is written; the word floats; "It joined the current. It was always part of it."
- **THE CURRENT:** 40 particles flow to his word drawn on the canvas with the six names drifting; "That pull — you may have called it obsession. Or distraction. Or being too much. / It was a Shakti — a living force — using the only language you could hear at that age: longing." → "The current flows toward your word. Your desire was the destination all along."
- **BODY:** "Where in your body does desire live? Not a specific desire. The capacity for desire itself. / Tap where it lives." Water ripples spread from the tap; **body point 2 'desire'**; "That place knows what it wants. It always has."
- **BREATH:** 2 × (4 in · 6 release) = 20 s → "The word you offered was already moving."
- **Binaural:** 144 Hz (D3) both ears; R 144 → 150 Hz (6 Hz) → 148 Hz (4 Hz); breath gain rises to .22 and falls to .08.
- **Sounding:** "Place your hand on your lower belly. Below the navel. / Hum VAM — let the sound be fluid, circular, slightly lower than comfortable. / Feel the movement below your hand."
- **RELEASE:** 0.5 s "Your Svadhisthana Chakra is now Breathing." (#E67E22) · "The force that pulled you toward everything you loved — it was pulling you toward yourself." / "It still is." · 15 s mini-reveal **"The lights you couldn't catch were leading too."** · 24 s **#D4A848** bleeds up · 32 s Bindu awakens · 35 s "watch".
- **Door copy:** "Six Lights Drift" — "Six lights move in the sacral field. The ones your hand is drawn to ignite. The ones that drift past were leading too."

### Dance — Crystallize (Lindsey Stirling)

- **Song:** **`tree-of-life/svadhisthana.mp3`** (Score *follow*, Track 24 `recegUJduJtuhYiOQ`).
- **Built legacy (`svadhisthana-dance(3).html`):** start overlay "svadhisthana" / pulsing dot / "feel"; seed **"You knew before you had the word for it."** typed one character per 857 ms (70 BPM), ~35 s, then "offer one word" · "then tap anywhere". DUR 258.8 s; PEAK_T 151; RETURN_START 18; DARK_RATIO .85 ("Water obscures."); 261 onset hits (peak 1.0 at 185.18 s, 27 hits > .6); E/B/P/T/EB/EM/EH/ED arrays, 518 samples at 0.5 s; one drop at 185.5 s (.624); MIRROR [33, 76, 119, 151, 210]. **Bindu orbits on three tides** (0–70 s; 70–130 breathing; 130–180 peak; 180–185 silence; 185–190 drop). First-tide ripple 33 s; drop ripple + haptic wave [30,20,30,20,30,20,30] at 185.5 s; hits > .6 haptic [20,15,20]; the offered word appears at 151 s and dissolves its letter-spacing over 3.5 s; "I Love You" at 156 s; **evaporation return** over the last 18 s (everything rises). Water language: bezier currents, a melodic ribbon, whirlpool, ribbon trails, gravity droplets. Movement type **TRAILS** ("ribbon trails = water"). Colour: from **#D47438** (the HTML's blue hue 220 yields to the Map).
- **Fruit (verbatim):** "There are no lyrics because you didn't need language to feel. Before your first word, you already knew joy, fear, warmth, cold. The violin is your body remembering what it knew before your mind took over." + motion weave + echo weave; closing "Trust what you felt just now. It was more accurate than anything you could say about it."; signature "svadhisthana · crystallize · lindsey stirling". Lalita's closing line: "Enjoy the water pretending it needs to be taught." One word: **Flow**.
- **Sound inside the dance only:** carrier D3 146.8 Hz (presence .15); EBB 1 80–115 s at 4 Hz ("deepest permission"); HOME = EBB 2 at 3 Hz, gain .045; the legacy "binaural" (one 144 Hz sine panned by gyro with a 3 Hz tremolo) is kept as the gyro-panned layer, not as the binaural (§7 C-10).
- **Memory:** the offered word joins his constellation of offered words — repeated words brighter (S.12). **Archetypes:** all named; Gaia leads.

### Gate — Textarea

- **Gate Text (verbatim):**
  > Place your hand on your lower belly — below the navel, above the hips.
  > Breathe into your hand. Let something move there.
  > When you feel the movement — tap.
  >
  > Button: I feel it moving
- **Behaviour:** the instruction drifts in on the current; an optional word without a box; the button is a **3200 ms fluid hold** (its ring ripples outward rather than filling); the word **is carved into the water and pools over 6.5 s** (padakarshini G32) and departs as an ember.
- **After-gate text (verbatim):**
  > Something moved.
  >
  > Not metaphorically. In the body. Below the navel, in the space where everything flows — desire, creativity, the tidal pull of what you want before you put it into words. You felt it moving because you stopped managing it for a moment and let it move.
  >
  > What moves in you is information. The direction of the movement is information. What it's moving toward is information.
  >
  > You have been taught to redirect, to channel, to moderate. All of that has its place. But first: feel the movement. Before the management. The management is downstream of the feeling. The feeling is the source.

### The teaching, revealed after the offering

- **Teaching Text** (3047 ch, 7 ¶): "Svadhisthana governs one irreducible fact: you want." … "The Kshobha Mudra is the readiness that lets you receive them without flinching." (the Matrikas paragraph — "It's emotional hospitality." — lights the eight seats kp 11–18 on the water).
- **"What the Sacral Knows That You Forgot"** (PET, 1742 ch, 8 ¶): "Following is what water does." … "The whole arc of your pulling — followed and unfollowed — was one continuous compass." (Its heading in the V5 template: "What the [Chakra] Knows That You Forgot".)

### What renders today

Five rings, text, RAM button, Textarea gate (field + 1.4 s), game doors Seed, Colour Rivers, Bhakti (G7-0365). **No Manipura page exists in the corpus** (`manipura-practice.html` is a GAP) — the register and the skills describe it fully (G3-0091, G3-0169), and Panel 3 and the Powerful dance are documented with numbers (G3-0093, G3-0194, G3-0220). Built here for the first time.

### Arrival

The crossing blooms in **#D4A848**; a heat haze breathes at the edges; **shortest sentences, fastest tempo** — the arrival lines come at 0.7× the usual spacing ("it hurts a little. That's fair."). Lines: **"the fire you feared was forging you"** → **"What am I for?"** → **"The fire was never wrong. What agitated was building."** Edges flicker in short strokes (register ◆). Crossing tone 55.00 Hz (ring 1).

### Story-as-motion

From the documented scene "The Forge" (G3-0169): embers, heat haze, **8 Śakti points**, RAM appearing; "Two trunks. One root." The point moves **gathering → first-ember → mekhala-lit → first-blaze → burning → breath → rebuild → forming → forging → second-blaze → malini-lit → recognition → seed-named → embers**. Each beat is **struck**, not tapped (G5 bearing, a strike every 0.9 s while held, G4-0820): the walker bears down and the forge answers. Timing: the Bindu story's constants (the source's "1.33× slower" note never took effect — kept as built). Signature **"The fire that agitated you was building your form."** → Veil → Act 2's last paragraph engraved: **"What comes next is the center that was waiting for the form to be stable enough to hold it. The sound that has been playing since before the pull. Since before the ground. Since before the stirring."** → the Anahata door in **#6B8E4E**: "Anahata / The sound is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line: "Something in the center is opening."

### Practice (held)

- **Burn** (Map Practice Prompt, a line per breath): "Stand. Navel slightly drawn toward the spine — not hard, just engaged. Hold that while breathing normally. That's the furnace acknowledging itself. You'll feel warmth arrive within a few breaths. / Now: what has the fire been building in you? And what is it burning that isn't yours? Write whichever surfaces first."
- **Numbers:** breath **3·3·6** ("fire, sharp inhale"), hold 2800 ms, trace *sharp*; the **Hold** form of time (padakarshini): a taut line across the room trembling 1.2 px while the navel is held.
- **Offering field:** "What has the fire been building in you — and what is it burning that isn't yours? Write whichever surfaces first. Or both." → "Both answers came from the same fire. Its building is also its burning. What it makes you into is also what it empties you of. One fire. Two movements."
- **Mudrā:** Kshobha, three breaths — "With each breath, release one thing from the furnace that belongs to someone else's agenda — an inherited timeline, an obligation that isn't yours, an expectation you've been burning for." Each breath, one ember leaves the room upward. Then: "Which of the 10 Mudra Shaktis is your body forming right now without you naming it? … The practice is not to perform a new gesture. The practice is to recognize the one already in motion. / The fire is not the problem. The question is always the fuel."
- **Three Ways In:** navel contraction · **Breath of Fire, thirty seconds** (a 30 s ring with rapid breath ticks) · "Eat one meal slowly. … The practice is to witness the furnace, not to do anything to it."
- **His Codex here:** action, self-confidence, procrastination as Manipura blocked (C-1198); release framed as gain, "it has to be something to get, not something to give up"; bhakti, seva and surrender as the key (C-1230). These shape the mudrā's wording-free motion (an ember given, never taken) — nothing is quoted on screen.

### Gate — Textarea

- **Gate Text (verbatim):**
  > Place your hand on your belly. Right at the navel.
  > Breathe in fully. Then breathe out completely.
  > Feel the heat that remains when the breath is gone.
  > When you feel the fire — tap.
  >
  > Button: I feel the fire
- **Behaviour:** an inhale ring fills, an exhale ring empties; at the empty point a **2800 ms sharp hold** ("I feel the fire"); the offered word (optional, no box) **is laid into the fire and flares from low over 5.5 s** (padakarshini G33); the alternative is G25 *join* — two words drawn together into a third (BUILD). The ember rises.
- **After-gate text (verbatim):**
  > The fire is real.
  >
  > Not a concept. A specific physical warmth behind the navel point. The place where food becomes fuel, where experience becomes action, where raw material passes through and comes out transformed. You placed your hand there and felt it.
  >
  > Everything passes through here. What you eat. What you experience. What you feel. It all goes into the sealed chamber and comes out as you. This is not passive. This is the most active thing happening in you at any moment.
  >
  > The fire is the maker. The maker is always working. You just paid attention to the making.

### What renders today

Five rings; text story; text practice **plus one held movement — heart, home ("Hṛdayam.", C-1174: a mote out and back to the heart)**; YAM button; Textarea gate; game doors Mirror Maze, Ram japa (G7-0366). The crossing blooms in **#6B8E4E**; **the space widens** — the room's margins open outward as you stay (padding grows by 4 pt per beat, from 44 pt), and an **optional 341 Hz under-tone** sits just below hearing (gain .004). What if they were doors?"** Design: "the first room that asks nothing and offers everything"; grace most often surfaces here (the architecture's grace layer). Crossing tone ring 2 root **61.74 Hz**.

### Story-as-motion

From the documented scene "The Heart" (G3-0170): a breath-glow; **14 triangles** (up gold, down green); the Sammohini label at the fullest breath; the heart draws itself; **eight birds lift**. The point moves **first-inhale → unstruck → second-inhale → opening → walls-doors → receiving → fullest-breath → recognition → overflow → dissolving → rippling**. Beats advance by **parting** (gesture G4, G4-0821): two fingers hold a veil open (r 104 px); the scene shows only through the opening. Signature **"The love that arrived for others was always your own heart opening."** → Veil → **"Speak."** → the Vishuddha door in **#4C7AB8**: "Vishuddha / The voice is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line: "The love wants to become sound."

### What renders today

Five rings; text; the Practice Prompt **plus two held movements — Ham · Het · Hum and roar and silence**; HAM button; Textarea gate; no game door (G7-0367). ### Arrival

The crossing blooms in **#4C7AB8**. **The only room whose shape constricts and releases:** the layout is narrow on arrival — the content column 62 % of the width, the walls of the room drawn in — and it **widens as he speaks or writes** (each sentence offered opens the column 6 % toward 100 %; BCA "speaking (writing) physically widens the page"). Lines: **"something in your throat has been waiting a long time to exit"** → **"Will I say what I know?"** → **"The space between the branches is as carefully designed as the branches themselves."** Crossing tone ring 3 root **69.30 Hz**. Lines fade at 420 ms here (the V5 Vishuddha exception).

### Story-as-motion

From `vishuddha-practice.html` (G3-0171): the **Bahir Dashara** figure (apex r 270, base r 180, θ ± 18°); a compressed spiral that **loosens** across **5 beats** (bdLevel 0 → 4); the Śaktis revealed as labels in order duhkhavimochini → mangalakarini + siddhiprada → sampatprada + priyankari → kamaprada; a 600 ms transition lock between beats. (A legacy comment says "11 beats across the song's seven phases" — the 5 built beats stand.) Beats advance by **asking and letting the exhale answer** (G36): he exhales audibly or holds the point through an exhale and the spiral loosens one level. Signature **"What you finally said out loud was what you always knew."** → Veil → **"Stop."** → the Ajna door in **#5B4E8E**: "Ajna / The seeing is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line: "Seeing without looking."

### Practice (held)

- **Say something true out loud** (Map Practice Prompt): "Say something true out loud. / Right now. To the empty room. Or the room that is not empty — it does not matter. The throat does not care who is listening. It needs you to hear it. / Not a confession. Not something dramatic. Something true. … / Say it. Hear your own voice say it. Notice what happens in your neck when the words leave."
- **Numbers:** breath **4·7·8** ("throat clearing"), hold 2400 ms, trace *reaching*. The mic listens for his voice (on device, never recorded); **voice luminance** — whisper = near-dark, roar = the room glows (EM → brightness, G4-0272).
- **Offering field:** "A small true thing. Or nothing at all — the throat knows what's ripening." → "The truth you offered was already formed. Your work was subtraction, not composition. The channel is clearer for the next one." Each sentence widens the room.
- **Mudrā:** Akarshana, three breaths; "This same mudra will return at Ajna … The Akarshana is one gesture, two registers: outward through the throat, inward through the eye." (chain 6·7).
- **Three Ways In:** "Sing." · "Read aloud." · "Tell someone something true that you have been holding. … The truth does not have to be hard. It just has to be true."
- **Held movements from his Codex** (built): **Ham · Het · Hum** — three breaths × three cycles at 4·4·4; Echo receives (HAM), Vishuddha holds (HET), Sonar transmits (HUM) (C-1342; AI-written vocabulary, his approval as an agenda); **roar and silence** — hold for the roar at 110 Hz with thumping haptics (roarHold 1.2 s) → release → 6 s of silence (C-1311: "the vishuddha chakra … silence … the mouthpiece is the roar").

### Wake-up — Panel 5 SPEAK (make any sound)

- **Opening hold 2400 ms.** Tone: **384 Hz** — "Clear, ringing, like a struck singing bowl. Voice frequency."
- **LOOSEN:** "Something has been held in your throat. / Let it loosen." Mic on; prompt "make any sound" (fallback "tap to loosen"). **35 lines held tight at 18 px spread toward .44 H**; leaves; voice waves. Mic FFT 256, smoothing .8; level += vol × .012 while vol > .05; each fallback tap +.14; **complete at ≥ .92** with a 5-leaf burst. "The space that was compressed was carrying your truth."
- **TRUTH:** "What did you finally say that had been true for a long time?" placeholder "your truth", button "speak" → **word 5**.
- **COST AND GIFT:** pivot "How long did you hold that before it came out?" / "The liberation of speaking is proportional to how long the truth was held. / Every year of silence added weight to the release."
- **BODY:** "Where is there tightness right now? / Not just the throat — anywhere." → "Make a sound — direct it there." / "Sound travels where words cannot." — waves travel to his tap when vol > .08, every 300 ms, max 12, 8 s timeout → **body point 5 'voice'**.
- **BREATH:** "Breathe from the throat. Not to release it. To clear the channel." 2 × 19 s → "What you most need to say is already being heard."
- **Binaural:** 384 Hz; R 390 (6 Hz) → 391 (7) → 392 (8).
- **Sounding:** "The throat is the instrument. No hand needed. / Hum HAM — feel the resonance specifically in the back of the throat where it meets the spine. / Then speak one true thing out loud."
- **RELEASE:** "Your Vishuddha Chakra is now Breathing." · "The voice you found was always yours. / It never needed permission." · 15 s mini-reveal **"You didn't find something to say. You finally stopped holding it."** · 24 s **#5B4E8E** bleeds · 32 / 35 s.

### Gate — Textarea, spoken

- **Gate Text (verbatim):**
  > Take a breath. Feel your throat — the passage where inside becomes outside.
  > Find the precise word for how you are right now. Not approximate. Precise.
  > Say it out loud.
  > When you have said it — tap.
  >
  > Button: I said it
- **Behaviour:** speak (padakarshini G46, on-device speech capture, nothing leaves the phone), with **typing beside** for a walker who cannot speak; the room brightens with his voice; "I said it" is a **2400 ms reaching hold**; the word (spoken or typed) lifts as an ember.
- **After-gate text (verbatim):**
  > You spoke. And the air changed.
  >
  > Not dramatically. But something moved. The thing that was inside is now outside — real in a different way than it was a moment ago. Named things have a different quality than unnamed things. The naming is not just description. The naming is a kind of becoming.
  >
  > The things that are hardest to say are not harder because they are worse. They are harder because they are more true. The throat knows this. The throat is not the obstacle. The throat is the location where truth finds its form.
  >
  > What you said today was always going to be said. The practice is only the occasion.

### What renders today

Five rings, text, OM button, Textarea gate, game door Still · Ajna (G7-0368). Documented outside: `ajna-practice.html` (story with two illuminations), Panel 6 (gyro stillness, constellation of his offerings), the first-pass `ajna-dance.html`.

### Arrival

The crossing blooms in **#5B4E8E** onto a deep-indigo starfield — **the dimmest energy room**. **Subtraction as form:** each arrival line removes something rather than adding (the starfield thins by a third with each line). Lines: **"what you tried to see has been watching you try"** → **"What do I see when I stop looking?"** → **"The seeing was always here. You were just looking too hard."** **Eyes-closed premieres here**: from this room on, a held two-finger touch lets the room continue with the screen dark. Crossing tone 69.30 Hz (ring 3).

### Story-as-motion

From `ajna-practice.html` (G3-0172): Shatkona r 160; the eye at the trunk's heart; six Śakti names in Devanagari; **a constellation of the five rooms before** (his body points 1–5 if he has given them); **two illuminations**. The point moves **dawning → ceased → first-illumination → eye-present → plateau → softening → constellation → om-present → second-illumination → stilling → ripples-peak → settling → mini-reveal → receiving → rippling** (closing timer 1500 ms). Beats advance by **stillness** (G2): the walker rests a finger and stops; after 1.5 s of stillness (≤ 4°/s rotation) the next beat comes; moving pauses it. Lines fade at 440 ms. Signature **"You stopped looking and the seeing arrived on its own."** → Veil → **"Receive."** → the Sahasrara door in **#9A7AC8**: "Sahasrara / The nothing is here. / Press · Continue →". Crossing line: "I think the name will come."

### Practice (held)

- **Sit in the dark behind your eyelids** (Map Practice Prompt): "Close your eyes. / Don't do anything. Don't meditate. Don't visualise. Don't try to see anything. / Just sit in the dark behind your eyelids. … / Wait. / Something will appear. … / What appeared?"
- **Numbers:** breath **6·0·6** ("even, witnessing"), hold 2000 ms, trace *light*. **The dark field** (Map gift #11): the screen goes to black for **two minutes**, no input, no line; a soft bell ends it (the existing bell); then the offering field. Each held breath before it removes one element of the room (Aperture form).
- **Offering field:** "What arrived in the dark when you stopped looking? Or nothing — the eye is patient." (placeholder: "You didn't make it appear. It was there before you looked."; returning: "The seeing from before. Still here. Add what arrived today, or leave the page as it was.") → "You didn't create what you saw. It was waiting for you to stop looking. The harder you'd been reaching, the longer it had been waiting. The eye that registers it is not the one you use to read these words."
- **Mudrā:** Akarshana turned inward, toward the brow ridge, three breaths: "Same mudra, two directions."
- **Three Ways In:** "Sit in the dark." · "Ask before thinking." · "Two minutes of nothing."

### Wake-up — Panel 6 STILL (hold the phone still)

- **Opening hold 2000 ms.** Tone: **426 Hz with a detuned twin beating at 1.5 Hz** — "Ethereal, shimmering, like two tones beating against each other." / "the eye opens slowly".
- **OPEN:** "Close your eyes for a moment. / Now open them — softly." · "hold your phone still". Gyro rotation averaged over 300 ms; **< 4.0°/s** counts as still; clarity +.25/s while still, −.12/s otherwise, floor .1; **complete at .80**; fallback "touch and hold"; 20 s safety timeout. Blur 18 × (1 − clarity) px with a warmth overlay. **14 seeded stars** (RNG seed 73); his **body points 1–5** joined by dashed lines; **words 2, 4, 5** visible once clarity > .6. "What becomes visible when effort stops." (Panel 6 reads; it writes nothing.)
- **PATTERN:** "You've been building this without seeing it. / Everything you offered — every word, every touch, every breath — was carried here by the eye that never closes. / Five points. Three words. One pattern. / You couldn't have seen it earlier. It required all five to become visible." (If he has given fewer, the line is shown only when all five exist — the pattern waits; nothing counts.)
- **WITNESS:** "Who is the one who sees the pattern? / Not the mind that names it. / The awareness that notices the mind naming."
- **SOFTEN:** "Soften your gaze. / Let your eyes see the way they see when you're about to fall asleep. / The screen is not showing you something. It is responding to you."
- **BREATH:** "Breathe into stillness. Not to find it. To see from it." 2 × 12 s; Dawn — the eyes open only on the inhales (G4-0823) → "Clarity was never something you achieved. It was what remained when effort stopped."
- **Binaural:** 426 Hz; R 430 (4 Hz) → 428 (2 Hz).
- **Sounding:** "Close your eyes. Hum AUM slowly — feel it in the space between your eyebrows. / At the end of each hum, hold the silence. / In the silence, look at the space between your brows from the inside."
- **RELEASE:** "Your Ajna Chakra is now Breathing." · "What you see when you stop looking was always there. / It never needed your effort." · 15 s mini-reveal **"You were being seen the entire time."** · 24 s **#9A7AC8** bleeds · 32 / 35 s.

### Dance — Experience (Ludovico Einaudi)

- **Song:** **`tree-of-life/ajna.mp3`** (Score *still-ajna*, Track 28 `rec3TqcK3UNBbqIxz`).
- **First-pass build (`ajna-dance.html`, no word, no binaural, no storage):** DUR 315.6 s; **Bindu perfectly still until 28 s**; piano onsets deposit stars; lines coloured by the journey's rooms **in reverse** (Vishuddha → Muladhara); LOVE_T 256; **a tear falls at 270 s**; fade at 300 s. "This song has no words. It doesn't need them." Movement type **CONSTELLATION** (each note deposits a hash-positioned star; lines draw over 1.5 s). The upgrade adds the VX loop the framework planned (seed → one word → mirror words → word at the peak → return → fruit) and the 426 Hz layer (G3-0044).
- **Fruit (verbatim):** "The piano gave you five minutes with nothing to think about and everything to feel... Experience. Not analyze. Not judge. Just experience. What did you see?" Lalita: "Still here. You always were." One word: **Open**. Phase names (from the Score): above 0 · adjusting 50 · first light 110 · held 155 · deepening 200 · seeing 235 · open 280 s.
- **Sound inside the dance only:** carrier C#3 138.6 Hz (presence .32); isochronic 1.1 Hz (.995) and 1.7 Hz (.990); "two illuminations, the second deeper, eye stays open." **Archetypes:** all named; **Sakshi leads**.
- **Door copy:** "Experience" — "Two illuminations. The second deeper. The eye stays open."

### Gate — Textarea, eyes closed

- **Gate Text (verbatim):**
  > Close your eyes. Let your gaze rest between your eyebrows — from the inside.
  > Not effortfully. Just rest there.
  > When something comes into focus — tap.
  >
  > Button: I’m looking
- **Behaviour:** **Still Point** stages (G10): each held stillness of 2000 ms takes one thing away from the screen (the type of the remaining line grows as the text shortens); when nothing is left but the button, "I’m looking" (the Map's words, kept although they rub against "stop looking" — §7 C-17) answers a single tap. The word, if any, lifts.
- **After-gate text (verbatim):**
  > You stopped looking.
  >
  > That is the practice. Not the looking — the stopping. The eye that sees most clearly is the one that stops trying to see and simply receives. You cannot force the third eye open any more than you can force a door by pushing from the wrong side. You can only stop pushing and wait for it to open from within.
  >
  > What you saw when you stopped looking — or what you almost saw, or what you sensed at the periphery — that is the register. Not the content of any specific vision. The quality of the register: clear, unhurried, available, receiving.
  >
  > You don’t see better by trying harder. You see better by stopping.

### The teaching, revealed after the offering

- **Teaching Text** (4274 ch, 11 ¶): "The Sanskrit means — command." … "The looking was the seeing, under the wrong name." ("The first five chakras each taught the walker a specific doing. Press. Follow. Burn. Open. Speak. The third eye says stop." — the five earlier rooms' verbs light in their colours as that paragraph reads.)
- **"What the Eye Knows That You Forgot"** (PET, 1924 ch, 6 ¶): "You were never the one seeing." … "The mini-reveal is that you were always inside the seeing."

### Doors

- **Mirror:** ↔ **Manipura (4)**. Incoming: Axis (18) names Ajna; Vision (17) sounds OM (Ajna's bīja) though its mirror door goes to Manipura (ruling 14).
- **Next:** → Sahasrara, "The nothing is here." · **Previous:** → Vishuddha. **Seats:** kp 49–52. **Ring 3 facts card.** **Road station:** "STILL · You stopped looking and the seeing arrived on its own." **Home game:** Still · Ajna. **Field:** "Song 7 of 33 — Two petals, one centered gaze."

## R08 · SAHASRARA — The Aperture
